\section{Functional Requirements}\label{sec:srs-9}
\texttt{AC}-01 verifies the exact stated behaviour; \texttt{AC}-02 verifies applicable rejection/\allowbreak{}permission paths. Apply only actions specified by the individual \texttt{FR}; the wider use case supplies context. Criteria are specifications, not recorded test results.

Name & Register \\[3pt]
Description & Customers register with email, password, full name and an optional phone number. The system sends a verification \texttt{OTP} and issues no token yet. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest; signed-in account; system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Register” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Customers register with email, password, full name and an optional phone number. The system sends a verification \texttt{OTP} and issues no token yet. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & An unverified registration and \texttt{OTP} delivery outcome are recorded; no access/\allowbreak{}refresh tokens are issued before verification. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Customers register with email, password, full name and an optional phone number. The system sends a verification \texttt{OTP} and issues no token yet. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Execute this operation: Sign in with email and password to get an access token (\texttt{JWT}, 60 minutes) and a refresh token (7 days). An unverified email gets \texttt{403} \texttt{EMAIL\_\allowbreak{}NOT\_\allowbreak{}VERIFIED\allowbreak{}}. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]

Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & Successful verified authentication stores the returned user/\allowbreak{}tokens and selects the allowed role landing; failure grants no session. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]

Name & Verify email \texttt{OTP} \\[3pt]
Description & Email verification with a 6-digit \texttt{OTP}: expires after 5 minutes, at most 5 attempts, 60 seconds between sends, a new code replaces the old one. Verification must include the registered password; on success the user is signed in and gets a welcome email. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest; signed-in account; system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Verify email \texttt{OTP}” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Email verification with a 6-digit \texttt{OTP}: expires after 5 minutes, at most 5 attempts, 60 seconds between sends, a new code replaces the old one. Verification must include the registered password; on success the user is signed in and gets a welcome email. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & Successful verified authentication stores the returned user/\allowbreak{}tokens and selects the allowed role landing; failure grants no session. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}07-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Email verification with a 6-digit \texttt{OTP}: expires after 5 minutes, at most 5 attempts, 60 seconds between sends, a new code replaces the old one. Verification must include the registered password; on success the user is signed in and gets a welcome email. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}07-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]
Evidence & \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/AuthService/AuthService/Controllers/AuthController.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Controll\allowbreak{}ers/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Controll\allowbreak{}er.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_Mobile/lib/features/auth/screens/verify\_email\_screen.dart}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}Mobile/\allowbreak{}lib/\allowbreak{}features\allowbreak{}/\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}screens/\allowbreak{}verify\_\allowbreak{}email\_\allowbreak{}screen.\allowbreak{}dart}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/AuthService/AuthService/Service/AuthService.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/ApiGateway/ApiGateway/Program.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Api\allowbreak{}Gateway/\allowbreak{}Api\allowbreak{}Gateway/\allowbreak{}Program.\allowbreak{}cs}} \\[3pt]

Execute this operation: Forgot password: an \texttt{OTP} is emailed (the \texttt{API} always returns 200); setting a new password with the \texttt{OTP} revokes every refresh token and marks the email as verified. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]

Name & Account field validation \\[3pt]
Description & The app and the \texttt{API} check account fields the same way (registration, sign-in, forgot /\allowbreak{} reset password, accounts created by Admin): email required, at most 150 characters, a domain with a suffix of at least 2 letters, no spaces; a new password of 6–72 characters counted in \texttt{UTF}-8 bytes; full name required, at most 150 characters; phone optional, 8–15 digits, at most 20 characters. Mistakes return \texttt{400} with an error per field. The sign-up form asks for the password twice. \\[3pt]

Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Account field validation” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]

Mistakes return \texttt{400} with an error per field. The sign-up form asks for the password twice. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]

A forgotten current password leads to the email \texttt{OTP} flow. Limited to 10 requests per minute like sign-in. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & M \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}17-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: \textbf{Change the password with the current one} (Account \ensuremath{\rightarrow} Change password): current password, new password twice. A wrong current password or the same password is refused (\texttt{400}). Afterwards other devices are signed out (refresh tokens revoked) and this device gets new tokens. A forgotten current password leads to the email \texttt{OTP} flow. Limited to 10 requests per minute like sign-in. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}17-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]
Evidence & \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/AuthService/AuthService/Controllers/AuthController.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Controll\allowbreak{}ers/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Controll\allowbreak{}er.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_Mobile/lib/features/profile/screens/profile\_screen.dart}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}Mobile/\allowbreak{}lib/\allowbreak{}features\allowbreak{}/\allowbreak{}profile/\allowbreak{}screens/\allowbreak{}profile\_\allowbreak{}screen.\allowbreak{}dart}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/AuthService/AuthService/Service/AuthService.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/ApiGateway/ApiGateway/Program.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Api\allowbreak{}Gateway/\allowbreak{}Api\allowbreak{}Gateway/\allowbreak{}Program.\allowbreak{}cs}} \\[3pt]

Name & Now showing and coming soon \\[3pt]
Description & The customer home has two tabs: \textbf{Now showing} (\texttt{ACTIVE} movies already released or without a release date, newest first) and \textbf{Coming soon} (\texttt{ACTIVE} movies released after today, earliest first, the release date on the poster). Movies can be filtered by genre (chips built from the genres of the movies on offer) and searched by title through the \texttt{API}, ignoring case. A movie coming soon shows its release date; without showtimes the page says they will be added closer to the release. \\[3pt]

Name & Movie reviews \\[3pt]
Description & \textbf{Movie reviews}: customers whose ticket was checked in (they watched the movie) rate it 1–5 stars with a comment (at most 1,000 characters) from the ticket page. One review per customer per movie: sending again edits it, and it can be deleted. A ticket not checked in is refused (\texttt{400}) with the reason; someone else's ticket is treated as missing (\texttt{404}). The movie page shows everyone the average, the count per star and the newest reviews; the reviewer name is the account's full name (or a masked email). Admins remove unsuitable reviews from Admin \ensuremath{\rightarrow} Movies \ensuremath{\rightarrow} Reviews. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest/\allowbreak{}Customer for browsing; Admin for catalogue changes and moderation \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A movie is available; changes need Admin; a review needs the owner’s checked-in ticket. \\[3pt]

Execute this operation: \textbf{Movie reviews}: customers whose ticket was checked in (they watched the movie) rate it 1–5 stars with a comment (at most 1,000 characters) from the ticket page. One review per customer per movie: sending again edits it, and it can be deleted. A ticket not checked in is refused (\texttt{400}) with the reason; someone else's ticket is treated as missing (\texttt{404}). The movie page shows everyone the average, the count per star and the newest reviews; the reviewer name is the account's full name (or a masked email). Admins remove unsuitable reviews from Admin \ensuremath{\rightarrow} Movies \ensuremath{\rightarrow} Reviews. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]

A ticket not checked in is refused (\texttt{400}) with the reason; someone else's ticket is treated as missing (\texttt{404}). The movie page shows everyone the average, the count per star and the newest reviews; the reviewer name is the account's full name (or a masked email). Admins remove unsuitable reviews from Admin \ensuremath{\rightarrow} Movies \ensuremath{\rightarrow} Reviews. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}MOV-\allowbreak{}12-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Screening format \\[3pt]
Description & Every showtime has a \textbf{format: 2D, 3D or \texttt{IMAX}} (2D by default, kept when left out on edit, anything else refused with \texttt{400}). The stored price is the standard seat at 2D; 3D adds 20,000 \texttt{VND} and \texttt{IMAX} 40,000 \texttt{VND} to the standard seat before the \texttt{VIP} /\allowbreak{} sweetbox surcharges. Seat prices shown to customers and charged by \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Service} include it. The app names the format next to the room (e.g. "Room 1 · \texttt{IMAX}"). \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest/\allowbreak{}Customer read public showtimes; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin manage \\[3pt]
Preconditions & Movie and room exist; management changes need Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Screening format” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Every showtime has a \textbf{format: 2D, 3D or \texttt{IMAX}} (2D by default, kept when left out on edit, anything else refused with \texttt{400}). The stored price is the standard seat at 2D; 3D adds 20,000 \texttt{VND} and \texttt{IMAX} 40,000 \texttt{VND} to the standard seat before the \texttt{VIP} /\allowbreak{} sweetbox surcharges. Seat prices shown to customers and charged by \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Service} include it. The app names the format next to the room (e.g. "Room 1 · \texttt{IMAX}"). 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject overlap with \texttt{409} and invalid time/\allowbreak{}format with \texttt{400}; cancelled showtimes are final. Customer selection excludes closed or started shows. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & M \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}SHOW-\allowbreak{}13-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Every showtime has a \textbf{format: 2D, 3D or \texttt{IMAX}} (2D by default, kept when left out on edit, anything else refused with \texttt{400}). The stored price is the standard seat at 2D; 3D adds 20,000 \texttt{VND} and \texttt{IMAX} 40,000 \texttt{VND} to the standard seat before the \texttt{VIP} /\allowbreak{} sweetbox surcharges. Seat prices shown to customers and charged by \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Service} include it. The app names the format next to the room (e.g. "Room 1 · \texttt{IMAX}"). \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}SHOW-\allowbreak{}13-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Hold booking \\[3pt]
Description & Create a booking from a showtime and seats: the showtime must be \texttt{OPEN} and not started, with at least one seat and no duplicates. The server takes the show time, movie title and prices from \texttt{Movie\allowbreak{}Service}. The booking is \texttt{PENDING} and holds the seats for 10 minutes. \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer owns online bookings; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin operate counter and administration \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A signed-in customer has selected an \texttt{OPEN} future showtime and seats; counter guests are represented by Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Hold booking” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Create a booking from a showtime and seats: the showtime must be \texttt{OPEN} and not started, with at least one seat and no duplicates. The server takes the show time, movie title and prices from \texttt{Movie\allowbreak{}Service}. The booking is \texttt{PENDING} and holds the seats for 10 minutes. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & A concurrent hold returns \texttt{409} and must not charge money or leave partial seats/\allowbreak{}points. Invalid concessions/\allowbreak{}code/\allowbreak{}points return validation errors. Expiry releases seats and restores reserved points. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & One \texttt{PENDING} booking with server total and \texttt{expires\allowbreak{}At} is committed; seats are \texttt{HELD} for ten minutes. A rejected transaction leaves no partial holds or point deduction. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Create a booking from a showtime and seats: the showtime must be \texttt{OPEN} and not started, with at least one seat and no duplicates. The server takes the show time, movie title and prices from \texttt{Movie\allowbreak{}Service}. The booking is \texttt{PENDING} and holds the seats for 10 minutes. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Concessions with tickets \\[3pt]
Description & \textbf{Snacks with tickets}: customers (booking summary) and the counter (sale screen) pick items from the menu, 1–20 of each, at most 10 different items. Prices come from the menu on sale; the snacks are part of the total, so payment, revenue and refunds include them. The order keeps the name and price at the time of purchase. Snacks show on the ticket, the payment page, the ticket email and the printed ticket. Stopped items are refused (\texttt{400}). \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer owns online bookings; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin operate counter and administration \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A signed-in customer has selected an \texttt{OPEN} future showtime and seats; counter guests are represented by Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin. \\[3pt]

Execute this operation: \textbf{Snacks with tickets}: customers (booking summary) and the counter (sale screen) pick items from the menu, 1–20 of each, at most 10 different items. Prices come from the menu on sale; the snacks are part of the total, so payment, revenue and refunds include them. The order keeps the name and price at the time of purchase. Snacks show on the ticket, the payment page, the ticket email and the printed ticket. Stopped items are refused (\texttt{400}). 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & A concurrent hold returns \texttt{409} and must not charge money or leave partial seats/\allowbreak{}points. Invalid concessions/\allowbreak{}code/\allowbreak{}points return validation errors. Expiry releases seats and restores reserved points. \\[3pt]

Prices come from the menu on sale; the snacks are part of the total, so payment, revenue and refunds include them. The order keeps the name and price at the time of purchase. Snacks show on the ticket, the payment page, the ticket email and the printed ticket. Stopped items are refused (\texttt{400}). \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}13-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Promotion and voucher \\[3pt]
Description & \textbf{Promotions}: only the ticket price is discounted (never snacks), by a percentage (optionally capped) or an amount, at most 90\% of the tickets, rounded down to 1,000 \texttt{VND}. Conditions: running period, day of the week of the show, minimum tickets, maximum uses. A promotion without a code applies by itself (the biggest discount wins); a promo code typed by the customer or the counter replaces it — one promotion per booking. A wrong or unusable code is refused (\texttt{400}) with the reason. The server works the discount out again when the booking is made; a use is only counted once the ticket is paid. The discount shows on the ticket, the payment page, the ticket email and the printed ticket. \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer owns online bookings; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin operate counter and administration \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A signed-in customer has selected an \texttt{OPEN} future showtime and seats; counter guests are represented by Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Promotion and voucher” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]

Conditions: running period, day of the week of the show, minimum tickets, maximum uses. A promotion without a code applies by itself (the biggest discount wins); a promo code typed by the customer or the counter replaces it — one promotion per booking. A wrong or unusable code is refused (\texttt{400}) with the reason. The server works the discount out again when the booking is made; a use is only counted once the ticket is paid. The discount shows on the ticket, the payment page, the ticket email and the printed ticket. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]

Name & Membership points \\[3pt]
Description & \textbf{Member points}: customers with an account earn points once a ticket is paid, on the amount actually paid and by tier: Member 5\%, \texttt{VIP} (from 2,000,000 \texttt{VND} spent) 7\%, \texttt{VVIP} (from 5,000,000 \texttt{VND}) 10\%; 1 point = 1,000 \texttt{VND}. When booking (app or counter) points can pay at most 50\% of the price (after promotions). An unpaid hold that expires or is cancelled gives the used points back; a paid ticket that is cancelled or refunded gives the used points back, takes the earned points back and lowers the total spent. Walk-in guests have no points. Customers see their member card (points, tier, what is left to the next tier, history) on the account page. \\[3pt]

Walk-in guests have no points. Customers see their member card (points, tier, what is left to the next tier, history) on the account page. 3. An unpaid hold that expires or is cancelled gives the used points back; a paid ticket that is cancelled or refunded gives the used points back, takes the earned points back and lowers the total spent. Walk-in guests have no points. Customers see their member card (points, tier, what is left to the next tier, history) on the account page. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}17-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Create/\allowbreak{}reuse \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} link \\[3pt]
Description & Create a \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} payment link for a \texttt{PENDING} booking. One payment per booking; asking again returns the existing link. The \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} order code is the booking id \ensuremath{\times} 1,000,000 plus the six random digits that end the booking code: stable per booking and never repeating an order of another environment on the shared \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} account. The link expires with the seat hold. \\[3pt]
Actor & Booking owner; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin for permitted operations; \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS}/\allowbreak{}system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A payable booking exists; \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} credentials and callback/\allowbreak{}return settings are configured for live payment. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Create/\allowbreak{}reuse \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} link” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Create a \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} payment link for a \texttt{PENDING} booking. One payment per booking; asking again returns the existing link. The \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} order code is the booking id \ensuremath{\times} 1,000,000 plus the six random digits that end the booking code: stable per booking and never repeating an order of another environment on the shared \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} account. The link expires with the seat hold. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Never trust redirect query parameters as proof of payment. Handle cancellation, timeout and \texttt{503} without showing false success; late payment confirms only if seats can be reclaimed, otherwise creates a refund. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}PAY-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Create a \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} payment link for a \texttt{PENDING} booking. The \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} order code is the booking id \ensuremath{\times} 1,000,000 plus the six random digits that end the booking code: stable per booking and never repeating an order of another environment on the shared \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} account. The link expires with the seat hold. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}PAY-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Late payment \\[3pt]
Description & When \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} reports payment after the hold expired, confirm the booking only if all seats can be reclaimed; otherwise create a full refund request. Repeated verification/\allowbreak{}webhook must not duplicate financial effects. \\[3pt]
Actor & Shared backend system; customer receives outcome \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The corresponding committed event, trusted \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} result or scheduled-worker condition exists; backend processing is configured. No customer session is required for a background worker or signed webhook. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Late payment” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: When \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} reports payment after the hold expired, confirm the booking only if all seats can be reclaimed; otherwise create a full refund request. Repeated verification/\allowbreak{}webhook must not duplicate financial effects. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Never trust redirect query parameters as proof of payment. Handle cancellation, timeout and \texttt{503} without showing false success; late payment confirms only if seats can be reclaimed, otherwise creates a refund. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & M \\[3pt]

Name & Create refund request \\[3pt]
Description & A refund request (\texttt{PENDING}) is created when: a customer cancels a paid ticket in time; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin cancel a paid ticket; a showtime is cancelled; a late payment lost its seats; an Admin refunds a payment manually. The payment becomes \texttt{REFUND\_\allowbreak{}PENDING} and the customer gets an email. \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer requests/\allowbreak{}reads own refund; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin process; system creates requests \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The corresponding committed event, trusted \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} result or scheduled-worker condition exists; backend processing is configured. No customer session is required for a background worker or signed webhook. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Create refund request” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: A refund request (\texttt{PENDING}) is created when: a customer cancels a paid ticket in time; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin cancel a paid ticket; a showtime is cancelled; a late payment lost its seats; an Admin refunds a payment manually. The payment becomes \texttt{REFUND\_\allowbreak{}PENDING} and the customer gets an email. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject too-late customer cancellation (\texttt{409}), completion without transfer reference and rejection without reason; cinema-cancelled refunds cannot be rejected. Duplicate events must not create another refund. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}REF-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: A refund request (\texttt{PENDING}) is created when: a customer cancels a paid ticket in time; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin cancel a paid ticket; a showtime is cancelled; a late payment lost its seats; an Admin refunds a payment manually. The payment becomes \texttt{REFUND\_\allowbreak{}PENDING} and the customer gets an email. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}REF-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Scan the \texttt{QR} code with a phone camera (app), a laptop webcam or \texttt{USB} camera (admin website), or type the code /\allowbreak{} use a handheld scanner (code + Enter). 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Keep manual entry available if camera is unavailable/\allowbreak{}denied. Unknown code returns \texttt{404}; unpaid, cancelled, pending refund, out-of-window or already-used ticket returns \texttt{409}. Concurrent second check-in fails. Name & Route guards and recovery \\[3pt]
Description & Guard named routes: a signed-out protected destination opens login with its pending route; successful sign-in resumes an allowed destination. Unknown routes fall back to the role landing. A summary lacking its in-memory \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Draft} returns to seat selection. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest; signed-in account; system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Route guards and recovery” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Guard named routes: a signed-out protected destination opens login with its pending route; successful sign-in resumes an allowed destination. Unknown routes fall back to the role landing. A summary lacking its in-memory \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Draft} returns to seat selection. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}NAV-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Guard named routes: a signed-out protected destination opens login with its pending route; successful sign-in resumes an allowed destination. Unknown routes fall back to the role landing. A summary lacking its in-memory \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Draft} returns to seat selection. \textbf{\texttt{WEB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}NAV-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]
